! Title: RespChla - Peliculas ONLINE GIGANTE V1
! Description: Bloqueo masivo de servicios legales y no autorizados de películas/series, mirrors, descargas, torrents y streaming web.
! Generated: 2026-07-31
! Syntax: AdGuard DNS blocklist (||domain^) + conservative regex rules
! Exact domains: 15,099
! Regex rules: 3
! Active blocking rules: 15,102
! Homepage: https://github.com/RespChla/SchoolList
!
! Sources:
! - UT1 audio-video + warez (CC BY-SA)
! - CommunityBlocklists movies (multiple upstream sources; see project licenses)
! - NextDNS piracy: streaming-video, torrent-websites, warez
! - BlockListProject piracy + torrent (MIT)
! - FMHY Streaming and Non-English indexes (current public catalogues)
! - Manual review of legal platforms, Spanish/Latin mirrors and rotating brands
!
! Safety:
! - Shared infrastructure roots (Google, YouTube, GitHub, Cloudflare, AWS, Vercel, Netlify, etc.) are not blocked wholesale.
! - Specific tenants on shared hosts may be blocked when identified as dedicated mirrors.
! - Core educational platforms are excluded rather than allowed with @@, so this file cannot override another intentional policy.
! - This profile intentionally blocks legitimate movie/series platforms such as Netflix, Disney+, Prime Video, Max and similar services.
! - YouTube and shared Google services are not blocked because DNS cannot isolate only movie paths without breaking educational use.
!
